Title: An Account of the Life and Death of Mr. Philip Henry, Minister of the Gospel near Whitchurch in Shropshire, who dyed June 24, 1696, in the sixty fifth year of his age; with Dr. Bates's dedication; in which is added a sermon preach'd on occasion of the death of his pious relict, Mrs. Katherine Henry, in the year 1707, by her son
Creator: Matthew Henry (1662-1714), the subject's son; with a dedication by Dr. William Bates
Publisher: London: printed for J. Lawrence and others (the names in the imprint are garbled in the scan; the host gives 'Printed for J. Lawrence')
Publication Date: 1712
Rights: Public Domain (published 1712, more than 95 years ago; public domain by date under the project's rolling rule; the item's own rights statement reads 'NOT_IN_COPYRIGHT')

Provenance note: Header prepended by the Library thread; the host's front matter up to and including the line beginning 'at |http : //books' removed (47 lines); nothing else altered, no OCR error corrected, nothing retyped. University of Michigan copy scanned by Google; Google's usage notice at the head is removed; clean OCR with the long s as f. The edition statement on the title page is garbled in the scan.
Content note: Matthew Henry's life of his father, one of the ministers ejected in 1662, who lived on at Broad Oak preaching privately and through the Indulgence; with the funeral sermon for his mother. The pattern-life of an ordinary ejected minister and his household, written by the son who became Dissent's great commentator.

Jefus, died November — 1695. about the Seventieth Year of
